<td valign="top"><strong>Question:</strong> Which interval in this protein corresponds to the Nudix box?<br><br><strong>Choices:</strong><br><br><strong>A.</strong> Residues 221–242<br><br><strong>B.</strong> Residues 251–272<br><br><strong>C.</strong> Residues 244–265<br><br><strong>D.</strong> Residues 353–374<br><br><strong>Flash-Next response:</strong><br><br><br><br>1. reasoning: To identify the Nudix box motif, I need to look for the characteristic pattern of this motif. The Nudix box is a conserved sequence motif found in Nudix hydrolases, characterized by multiple conserved glutamate (E) residues followed by a critical aspartate (D) residue, typically with the consensus pattern containing the arrangement: E-x-N/A-S/V/T-E/Q/D-N/K/S-R/C/L-S/T-X-D-X-X.<br><br>Let me examine each option:<br><br>- **Option A (221-242):** &quot;PTNEHALLVRHRGSAGGVFTAV&quot; — Contains only 1 E and no D. Does not match the Nudix pattern.<br><br>- **Option B (251-272):** &quot;SMAECARREIAEEVGIEVDSIR&quot; — Contains multiple conserved E residues (positions 254, 259, 262, 263, 267) and a critical D residue (position 269). This E-x₄-E-x₂-EE-x₃-E-x₁-D pattern is highly characteristic of the Nudix box. The catalytic aspartate at position 269 is included, which is essential for metal coordination in Nudix hydrolases.<br><br>- **Option C (244-265):** &quot;GFAHSGESMAECARREIAEEVG&quot; — Contains E residues (250, 254, 259, 262, 263) but critically **excludes** the essential D residue at position 269. Actually, let me reconsider the Nudix motif definition more carefully.

The Nudix motif was defined by McClellan et al. (2002) as a 23-residue segment:
N5-E5-U6-X7-R8-X9

Where:
- N5 = positions 1-5: region enriched in N and G
- E5 = positions 6-10: region enriched in E
- U6 = position 11: hydrophobic residue (V, I, or L)
- X7 = positions 12-18: 7 variable residues
- R8 = position 19: conserved Arg
- X9 = positions 20-23: 4 variable residues

Wait, let me recount. 5+5+1+7+1+4 = 23. Yes, 23 residues.

So the pattern is:
Positions 1-5: N/G-rich
Position 6-10: E-rich
Position 11: Hydrophobic (V/I/L)
Positions 12-18: Variable
Position 19: Arg
Positions 20-23: Variable
